     1→# ADR #20: Extensions Package for Contributed Components
     2→
     3→**Date**: 2026-03-09
     4→**Decision Makers**: Antonín Teichmann
     5→
     6→**Related**: [ADR-10] (Versioning), [ADR-11] (Component Governance), [ADR-19] (Customization Strategy)
     7→
     8→## Context and Problem Statement
     9→
    10→Multiple product teams (Recommendations, Pricing/Promotions) need to share React components across CustomInk applications. These components are already in production — product cards, carousels, promo banners — but are duplicated across codebases, leading to inconsistent UX and repeated implementation effort.
    11→
    12→The teams want to use Pigment's infrastructure (monorepo CI, Storybook, Chromatic/Argos, design tokens) to distribute their components. However, some of these are product-level components that may contain business logic, data fetching hooks, and domain-specific APIs — fundamentally different from Pigment's presentational, domain-agnostic primitives.
    13→
    14→
    15→## Decision Drivers
    16→
    17→- **Release independence**: Contributing teams must ship on their own cadence without blocking or being blocked by Pigment releases
    18→- **Ownership clarity**: No ambiguity about who reviews, maintains, and releases what
    19→- **Architectural boundary**: Pigment (`@customink/pigment-react`) remains presentational only; no hooks, fetching, or business logic
    20→- **Infrastructure reuse**: Contributing teams benefit from Pigment's CI pipeline, Storybook, visual regression testing, and design tokens without coupling to Pigment's npm package
    21→- **Reduce DS as bottleneck**: DS review scope must be narrow and well-defined so contributing teams are not waiting on DS for most changes
    22→
    23→## Considered Options
    24→
    25→### 1. Contributed directory inside `@customink/pigment`
    26→
    27→Components in `packages/react/src/contributed/{team-name}/`, shipped as part of the `@customink/pigment` npm package.
    28→
    29→**Pros:**
    30→- Single import for consumers (`import { X } from '@customink/pigment'`)
    31→- No new package to set up
    32→
    33→**Cons:**
    34→- Release coupling — a contributed component bug blocks Pigment releases
    35→- Semver coupling — contributing teams must coordinate breaking changes with DS
    36→- Separated package needed for hooks, AB testing, data fetching and business logic
    37→- DS becomes review bottleneck for all public API changes
    38→
    39→### 2. Separate repository per team
    40→
    41→Each contributing team maintains their own repo with Pigment as a dependency.
    42→
    43→**Pros:**
    44→- Complete independence
    45→
    46→**Cons:**
    47→- No shared CI pipeline, Storybook, or visual regression infrastructure
    48→- Each team must set up and maintain their own build/test/publish tooling
    49→- Component documentation scattered across repos
    50→- No single Storybook for discovery
    51→
    52→### 3. Separate package in the Pigment monorepo
    53→
    54→A new package `@customink/pigment-extensions` in `packages/extensions/`, with `@customink/pigment` as peer dependency (ranged). Same monorepo, independent npm package.
    55→
    56→**Pros:**
    57→- Release decoupling — independent versioning and release cadence
    58→- Shared infrastructure — same CI, Storybook, Chromatic/Litmus, Argos, e2e tests, publish process
    59→- Clean architectural boundary — extensions may contain hooks, business logic, state
    60→- Narrow DS review scope — DS reviews infra concerns only, not component logic
    61→- CODEOWNERS at package level — no ambiguity
    62→
    63→**Cons:**
    64→- Separate import path for consumers (`@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`)
    65→- New package setup (one-time cost)
    66→
    67→## Decision Outcome
    68→
    69→**Chosen: Option 3 — Separate package `@customink/pigment-extensions` in the Pigment monorepo.**
    70→
    71→### Package structure
    72→
    73→```
    74→packages/
    75→  extensions/
    76→    package.json              # peers on pigment-react. Exports map has one entry per team (e.g. ./reco, ./pricing), pointing to team's index
    77→    tsconfig.json
    78→    reco/                     # Recommendations team
    79→      TrendingProductsWidget/
    80→      ...
    81→    pricing/                  # Pricing/Promotions team
    82→      PromoBanner/
    83→      ...
    84→```
    85→
    86→### CODEOWNERS
    87→
    88→Rules are added to the repo-level `.github/CODEOWNERS` (GitHub ignores CODEOWNERS files in subdirectories):
    89→
    90→```
    91→packages/extensions/                          @customink/design-system  # the root, explicit
    92→packages/extensions/{team-name}               @customink/{team-name}    # line per-team
    93→
    94→apps/storybook/stories/extensions/                    @customink/design-system
    95→apps/storybook/stories/extensions/{team-name}         @customink/{team-name}    # line per-team
    96→```
    97→
    98→DS owns the package root (package.json, tsconfig, build config, CI setup). Team-specific paths override for their directories.
    99→
   100→### Storybook
   101→
   102→Stories live in `apps/storybook/stories/extensions/{team-name}/`, mirroring the package structure. Same CODEOWNERS pattern: DS owns the `extensions/` root (approves new team directories). Teams own their subdirectories — no DS code review on story content. Storybook build is enforced by CI.
   103→
   104→### What DS enforces (infrastructure concerns)
   105→
   106→These are non-negotiable and mostly enforced by the shared CI pipeline and DS code review:
   107→
   108→- **Design tokens via CSS custom properties** — no hardcoded visual values. This is what makes it visually Pigment.
   109→- **`@customink/pigment` as peer dependency**
   110→- **Storybook stories exist** — every component has a story. This is the documentation contract.
   111→- **No new npm dependencies without DS review** — supply chain concern for a published package.
   112→
   113→Everything enforced by TypeScript strict mode, ESLint, Prettier, automated claude-based CR, and the existing CI gates applies automatically. If the code passes CI, it meets the infrastructure bar.
   114→
   115→**Visual regression from upstream changes:** In the monorepo, extensions always run against the latest `pigment-react` source (via `file:` protocol). This means a visual change to a Pigment primitive (e.g., Button padding) will immediately produce Chromatic/Argos diffs for any extension story that uses that primitive. Contributing teams own approval of their visual diffs, including diffs caused by upstream changes. DS will communicate planned visual changes, but the approval responsibility stays with the extension team. This is acceptable at current scale (2 contributing teams). If the number of extension stories grows to a point where upstream visual changes routinely block `main`, we will re-evaluate (e.g., separate Chromatic projects per team, TurboSnap scoping).
   116→
   117→### What DS does NOT review
   118→
   119→Component logic, prop API design, internal folder structure, hook implementations, business logic, state management, styling approach (ADR-19 applies to `@customink/pigment-react` only, not extensions). Usage of pigment-react v0 (via the backwards compatibility import) vs v1. Contributing teams own these decisions entirely.
   120→
   121→### What contributing teams own
   122→
   123→Their components, their tests, their stories, their releases, their bugs. Team-specific Claude Code skills and commands go in the team's directory. Standard incident response if something breaks `main`.
   124→
   125→### Business logic
   126→
   127→Unlike `@customink/pigment` (presentational only), `@customink/pigment-extensions` may contain hooks, data fetching, state management, and domain-specific logic. This is the core architectural distinction: Pigment is the design system, extensions are product code built on the design system.
   128→
   129→### Atom backfilling
   130→
   131→Contributing teams may need v1 atoms that don't exist yet (e.g., Carousel, Tooltip, Popover). These can be implemented as local, widget-scoped components inside the team's directory — not exported from the package public API. When DS implements the official v1 atom, the team updates their import and deletes the local copy. This graduation path is tracked via Jira following the standard process.
   132→
   133→### Versioning and releases
   134→
   135→`@customink/pigment-extensions` has its own semver, independent from `@customink/pigment`. Contributing teams coordinate breaking changes among themselves. DS owns the release pipeline and `package.json`.
   136→
   137→**Target state:** Contributing teams publish independently without DS involvement.
   138→
   139→**Current blocker:** Lerna publishes all packages in a single run and requires semver decisions for every changed package. This means a contributing team cannot publish without triggering a Pigment release decision.
   140→
   141→**Interim process (until tooling migration):**
   142→- Contributing teams coordinate with DS before publishing.
   143→- DS response SLA: max 2 business days, ideally 1.
   144→- DS team to evaluate and migrate to a per-package publish tool (e.g., Changesets). Tracked in [Jira](https://customink.atlassian.net/browse/DS-323), planned for over-next sprint (~1 month).
   145→
   146→### Figma alignment
   147→
   148→Contributing teams should ensure their component designs are added to the **Figma Pigment Global Components > Sandbox** library. This aligns the design-side exploration space with the code-side extensions package. The mapping is not necessarily 1:1 but serves a similar purpose: a staging area for components that may eventually graduate to Pigment proper.
   149→
   150→### Graduation path
   151→
   152→When a contributed component matures into a company-wide design system component, it can be moved to `@customink/pigment-react` under DS ownership. This follows the existing Component Governance process ([ADR-11]) and the graduation criteria documented in the [Component Inclusion & Graduation pilot](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1nYYplOXC33jUw2W3EkPj9FOQRrooSmcSqbuwBiVNAHQ/edit). Graduation is a separate decision per component, not automatic.
   153→
   154→**Scope of each package:**
   155→- `@customink/pigment-react` is home to any **domain-agnostic, presentational** component — atoms, molecules, or organisms. The boundary is not complexity level but ownership and domain: DS-owned, no business logic, no data fetching.
   156→- `@customink/pigment-extensions` can host **anything reusable** across apps — domain-agnostic or domain-specific, any complexity level. Contributing teams decide their own internal structure and atomic design tier.
   157→- As a natural side effect, domain-agnostic components in extensions can be promoted to `pigment-react` if both the contributing team and DS agree. Domain-specific components (with business logic, hooks, state) stay in extensions permanently. This is expected and fine.
   158→
   159→## Consequences
   160→
   161→
   162→### Positive
   163→- Contributing teams release independently — no DS bottleneck
   164→- Pigment releases are not blocked by contributed component bugs
   165→- Clean architectural boundary between design system and product code
   166→- Shared CI, Storybook, and visual regression infrastructure without coupling
   167→- DS review scope is narrow and fast (infra only)
   168→- Single Storybook for component discovery across DS and extensions
   169→
   170→### Negative
   171→- Consumers need two imports (`@customink/pigment-react` and `@customink/pigment-extensions`)
   172→- One-time setup cost for the new package
   173→- Contributing teams must coordinate breaking changes among themselves within the shared package
   174→
   175→### Future considerations
   176→- If the number of contributing teams grows significantly, evaluate whether the single extensions package should split per team
   177→- `packages/react` may be renamed to `packages/core` for clarity (separate change, not part of this ADR)
   178→- Automated bundle impact checks in CI (in backlog)
   179→
   180→[ADR-10]: ./10-Component-Library-Versioning.md
   181→[ADR-11]: ./11-Component-Governance.md
   182→[ADR-19]: ./19-Component-Customization-Strategy.md

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

     1→# Contributing to @customink/pigment-extensions
     2→
     3→This guide covers how product teams add and maintain components in the extensions package.
     4→
     5→## Prerequisites
     6→
     7→Consumers of your components must already have `@customink/pigment-react` set up in their application. This means:
     8→
     9→- `@customink/pigment-react` is installed
    10→- The base CSS import is present (e.g., `import '@customink/pigment-react/base.css'`), which loads design token CSS custom properties
    11→
    12→Since `@customink/pigment-extensions` peers on `@customink/pigment-react`, all design tokens are already available via CSS custom properties in the consuming application. You do not need to import token CSS separately.
    13→
    14→## Team directory structure
    15→
    16→Each team owns a top-level directory in the package, each having an `index.ts` barrel export that re-exports all public components.
    17→
    18→```
    19→packages/extensions/
    20→  storefront/              # Storefront team
    21→    ComponentName/
    22→      ComponentName.tsx    # Just a suggestion for consistencu. Internals are team-owned.
    23→      index.ts
    24→    index.ts               # Barrel export for the team
    25→    README.md              # Optional: team-specific docs
    26→    CONTRIBUTING.md        # Optional: team-specific contribution guide
    27→  reco/                    # Recommendations team
    28→    index.ts
    29→  promotions/              # Promotions team
    30→    index.ts
    31→```
    32→
    33→Teams can add their own `README.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md` inside their directory for team-specific setup instructions, conventions, or onboarding notes. The DS does not review these files.
    34→
    35→## What the Design System enforces
    36→
    37→These are non-negotiable requirements from [ADR-20](../../docs/adr/20-Extensions-Package.md):
    38→
    39→1. **Design tokens via CSS custom properties** - no hardcoded color, spacing, or typography values. Use `var(--token-name)` or component props for all visual properties, unless not possible. This is what makes your component visually consistent with Pigment.
    40→2. **`@customink/pigment-react` as peer dependency** - use Pigment components and tokens, do not bundle them.
    41→3. **Storybook stories required** - every public component must have a story. This is the documentation contract.
    42→4. **No new npm dependencies without DS review** - supply chain concern for a published package. If you need a new dependency, open a PR and tag the DS team.
    43→
    44→## What teams own
    45→
    46→Contributing teams have full ownership over:
    47→
    48→- Component logic and implementation
    49→- Prop API design
    50→- Hooks, data fetching, state management
    51→- Business logic
    52→- Styling approach (inline styles, CSS modules, etc.)
    53→- Internal folder structure within the team directory
    54→- Tests and test strategy
    55→
    56→## Storybook stories
    57→
    58→Stories for extension components go in the Storybook app, under a team-specific directory:
    59→
    60→```
    61→apps/storybook/stories/extensions/{team-name}/
    62→```
    63→
    64→For example, a story for the Storefront team's TestingBanner would be at:
    65→```
    66→apps/storybook/stories/extensions/storefront/TestingBanner.stories.tsx
    67→```
    68→
    69→Follow the same story patterns used in the main Pigment Storybook stories.
    70→
    71→## Building
    72→
    73→From the package root (`packages/extensions/`):
    74→
    75→```bash
    76→npm run build        # Compile TypeScript to lib/
    77→npm run typecheck    # Type-check without emitting
    78→npm run lint:check   # Run ESLint
    79→npm run lint:fix     # Run ESLint with auto-fix
    80→```
    81→
    82→The build uses `tsc` with `tsconfig.build.json` and outputs to `lib/`.
    83→
    84→## Testing
    85→
    86→Teams can add tests following the same patterns as `@customink/pigment-react`. Recommended approach: Place test files alongside your components (e.g., `ComponentName.test.tsx`). Test files are excluded from the build output.
    87→
    88→## Claude Code integration
    89→
    90→Teams can customize their Claude Code experience in two ways:
    91→
    92→### Team-specific instructions (CLAUDE.md)
    93→
    94→Add a `CLAUDE.md` file in your team directory (e.g., `packages/extensions/reco/CLAUDE.md`). Claude Code automatically loads it when working on files in that directory. Use it for team-specific coding conventions, context, or instructions.
    95→
    96→### Team-specific slash commands
    97→
    98→Each team has a dedicated directory for Claude Code slash commands:
    99→
   100→```
   101→.claude/commands/reco/          # /reco-{command-name}
   102→.claude/commands/storefront/    # /storefront-{command-name}
   103→.claude/commands/promotions/    # /promotions-{command-name}
   104→```
   105→
   106→Commands are invoked as `/{team}-{command-name}` (e.g., a file `.claude/commands/reco/build-widget.md` becomes `/reco-build-widget`). These directories are owned by the respective teams in CODEOWNERS - no DS review needed.
   107→
   108→## Adding a new team
   109→
   110→To onboard a new team:
   111→
   112→1. Create a new directory under `packages/extensions/` (e.g., `packages/extensions/my-team/`)
   113→2. Add an `index.ts` barrel export in the new directory
   114→3. Add an entry to the `exports` map in `package.json`
   115→4. Add CODEOWNERS entries for the new team directory (package, storybook stories, and Claude commands)
   116→5. Create the corresponding Storybook stories directory at `apps/storybook/stories/extensions/my-team/`
   117→6. Create the Claude commands directory at `.claude/commands/my-team/`
   118→7. Open a PR and tag the DS team for review

Got everything. Here's the full picture:

---

## Summary

### Where the widget goes

The `TrendingProductsWidget` belongs in `packages/extensions/reco/` — owned by the Recommendations team.

```
packages/extensions/
  reco/
    TrendingProductsWidget/
      TrendingProductsWidget.tsx
      TrendingProductsWidget.module.css   # (if CSS modules)
      TrendingProductsWidget.test.tsx
      index.ts
    index.ts                              # barrel export
```

Stories go in:
```
apps/storybook/stories/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget.stories.tsx
```

### Key constraints (DS-enforced)

1. **No hardcoded visual values** — use `var(--token-name)` CSS custom properties only
2. **`@customink/pigment-react` as peer dep** — don't bundle it
3. **Storybook story required** — it's the documentation contract
4. **No new npm deps without DS review**

### What the reco team owns freely

- Component logic, hooks, data fetching, business logic
- Prop API design
- Internal structure within `reco/`
- Tests strategy

### Exports

`package.json` already has `"./reco"` export pointing to `./lib/reco/index.js`. Just need to export the widget from `reco/index.ts`.

### Atom backfilling note

If the widget needs v1 atoms that don't exist yet (Carousel, Tooltip, etc.), create local copies inside `reco/` — not exported from the public API. When DS ships the official atom, swap the import and delete the local copy.

@@ -0,0 +1,695 @@
+# RFC 1: Trending Products Widget Migration
+
+**Date**: 2026-03-04
+**Decision Makers**: Tonda (Antonín) Teichmann, Josef Zamrzla, Andrii Popenko
+
+## Context and Problem Statement
+  
+Right now we have similar widgets for recommendations that we use for across different applications in Custom Ink ecosystem. To avoid code duplication and to speed up development during expansion to other apps we decided to move source code to the `Pigment` repo and re-use widget as plug-in ready-to-use peace of functionality.
+
+Examples of widgets:
+
+ 1. Trending products widget on categories pages;
+  ![Trending products widget on categories pages](./rfc-assets/1-trending-products-widget-migration-img-1.png)
+ 2. Trending products widget on Favorites page (WIP).
+  ![Trending products widget on Favorites page](./rfc-assets/1-trending-products-widget-migration-img-4.png)
+
+They look lookalike  with small differences that can be handled by props flags.
+
+## Decision Drivers
+  
+  Key factors influencing the decision:
+  
+- We'd like to avoid code duplication;
+- We want to avoid bugs during copy-pasting and re-implementing across apps;
+- to speed up development and launch experiments with similar widgets faster. As result iterate faster with improvements.
+
+## Solution
+  
+### 1. New folder for recommendations widgets
+
+In `packages/react`, we need to create a new folder `widgets` where teams can create widget folders for their purposes.
+
+```plaintext
+react/
+├── widgets/
+│   ├── RecommendationsWidgets/              # Reco team widgets
+│   │   ├── TrendingProductsWidget/
+│   │   │   ├── TrendingProductsWidget.tsx    # Widget component
+│   │   │   ├── useTrendingProducts.ts        # hook for handling data loading
+│   │   │   ├── types.ts                      # TypeScript types
+│   │   │   ├── index.ts                      # Public exports
+│   │   │       ├── Atoms/                        # Single-purpose primitives; no product-domain knowledge
+│   │   │       │   ├── Carousel/
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── Carousel.tsx
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── Carousel.module.css
+│   │   │       │   │   └── index.ts
+│   │   │       │   ├── FavoriteButton/
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── FavoriteButton.tsx
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── FavoriteButton.module.css
+│   │   │       │   │   └── index.ts
+│   │   │       │   ├── Tooltip/
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── Tooltip.tsx
+│   │   │       │   │   ├── Tooltip.module.css
+│   │   │       │   │   └── index.ts
+│   │   │       │   └── Popover/
+│   │   │       │       ├── Popover.tsx
+│   │   │       │       ├── Popover.module.css
+│   │   │       │       └── index.ts
+│   │   │       └── Molecules/                    # Composed of atoms; has product-domain knowledge
+│   │   │           └── RecommendationsProductCard/
+│   │   │               ├── RecommendationsProductCard.tsx
+│   │   │               ├── RecommendationsProductCard.module.css
+│   │   │               └── index.ts
+```
+
+**Public exports (`index.ts`):**
+
+```ts
+// packages/react/src/widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget/index.ts
+export { TrendingProductsWidget } from './TrendingProductsWidget';
+export { useTrendingProducts } from './useTrendingProducts';
+export type {
+  TrendingProduct,
+  TrendingProductsWidgetProps,
+} from './types';
+
+// Atoms and Molecules are NOT exported from the widget's public index during the fast-track phase.
+// They are accessible via deep import for internal widget use only:
+// import { FavoriteButton } from '.../Atoms/FavoriteButton';
+// import { RecommendationsProductCard } from '.../Molecules/RecommendationsProductCard';
+```
+
+The widget is re-exported from the main package index:
+
+```ts
+// packages/react/src/index.ts
+export { TrendingProductsWidget, useTrendingProducts } from './widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget';
+export type { TrendingProduct, TrendingProductsWidgetProps } from './widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget';
+```
+
+### 2. Widget decomposition
+
+To assemble a trending products widget, we need the following components organized by atomic design tier.
+
+**Existing v1 (no work needed):**
+- [x] `Typography`
+- [x] `Link`
+- [x] `Box`
+
+**Atoms (to implement in `Atoms/`):**
+- [ ] `Carousel` — exists in v0 (MUI), reimplement in v1 with keen-slider.
+- [ ] `FavoriteButton` — exists in catalog, reimplement as presentational atom without MUI.
+- [ ] `Tooltip` — exists in v0 (MUI), reimplement with `@floating-ui/react`. (used by FavoriteButton)
+- [ ] `Popover` — exists in v0 (MUI), reimplement with `@floating-ui/react`. (used for pricing details)
+
+**Molecules (to implement in `Molecules/`):**
+- [ ] `RecommendationsProductCard` — exists in v0 (MUI), reimplement from catalog `RelatedProductCard`. Provisional name; likely becomes a variant of a future `ProductCard` atom.
+
+**No standalone `ImageWrapper` needed** — the image area is card-specific layout handled inside `RecommendationsProductCard`.
+
+### 3. Components Backfilling
+
+The normal Design System flow for a new component is:
+
+> Reco team creates Jira task → Designers create spec in Figma → Design System team reviews and implements → component is published → consumers use it
+
+This flow ensures quality and consistency but takes time. Waiting for it would block the widget migration.
+
+**Fast-track decision:** The Recommendations team will implement the required components directly inside the widget's `Atoms/` and `Molecules/` directories (see folder structure in section 1). These are local, widget-scoped implementations — not part of the Pigment public API yet.
+
+When the Design System team is ready to promote a component to the library, they can use the widget's implementation as a starting point rather than building from scratch.
+
+**Semver impact:**
+
+Widget code lives inside `packages/react`, so all changes increment Pigment's semver. **The Recommendations team must coordinate with the Design System team before merging breaking changes to the widget API. Non-breaking additions (new optional props, new components) can be merged independently under a minor bump.**
+
+**Responsibilities:**
+
+- **Recommendations team** — implements components in `Atoms/` and `Molecules/`, keeps them isolated inside the widget folder, creates a Jira task per component and labels it as tech debt.
+- **Design System team** — picks up the Jira tasks on their normal schedule, creates the Figma spec, and implements the official v1 component in `packages/react/src/components/`. Once the official component lands, the widget import is updated and the local copy removed.
+
+**Exit criteria (when a component graduates to the public library):**
+
+1. A Figma spec exists and has been reviewed by the Design System team.
+2. The official v1 implementation passes lint, type check, and unit tests.
+3. A Storybook story is published.
+4. The widget's internal import is updated to use the public component.
+5. The local `Atoms/<Name>/` or `Molecules/<Name>/` folder is deleted.
+6. The Jira tech-debt ticket is closed.
+
+**Tech debt tasks to create (one per component):**
+
+| Component | Tier | Jira label | Notes |
+|-----------|------|-----------|-------|
+| `Carousel` | Atom | `tech-debt`, `pigment-atom` | Replace local impl with official v1 once published |
+| `FavoriteButton` | Atom | `tech-debt`, `pigment-atom` | Presentational only; state stays in catalog |
+| `Tooltip` | Atom | `tech-debt`, `pigment-atom` | Needed by FavoriteButton |
+| `Popover` | Atom | `tech-debt`, `pigment-atom` | Needed for price breakdown |
+| `RecommendationsProductCard` | Molecule | `tech-debt`, `pigment-molecule` | Likely becomes a variant of a future `ProductCard` |
+
+### 4. Component Contracts
+
+> **Implementation requirement:** All components (atoms and molecules) must use `React.forwardRef`. This is a non-negotiable pattern in Pigment v1 (see `docs/ai/existing-patterns/components.md`). Each component must also set `displayName` for DevTools.
+
+---
+
+#### 4.1 Atoms
+
+Single-purpose primitives with no product-domain knowledge. Can be composed into molecules.
+
+##### ImageWrapper
+
+**Decision: no standalone `ImageWrapper` component needed.**
+
+The image area in `RecommendationsProductCard` is a `<div>` with `position: relative`, aspect-ratio padding, overflow hidden, and an inner absolutely-positioned `<img>`. This is card-specific layout, not a reusable atom. If the same pattern emerges across unrelated components, extract then.
+
+---
+
+##### Carousel
+
+The v0 `Carousel` is general-purpose (dots, thumbnails, navigator panel). `TrendingCarousel.jsx` skips it because it needs `'auto'` slide-width mode and dynamic `slidesPerView` computed from product count. The v1 is a focused reimplementation: keen-slider, no MUI, arrows only.
+
+```ts
+type CarouselProps = {
+  children: React.ReactNode;
+  slidesPerView?: number | 'auto';
+  spacing?: number;
+  loop?: boolean;
+  arrows?: boolean;
+  onPreviousClick?: () => void;
+  onNextClick?: () => void;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+
+```tsx
+<Carousel slidesPerView={4} spacing={16} loop arrows>
+  {productCards}
+</Carousel>
+
+{/* With auto width — caller controls slide width via CSS */}
+<Carousel slidesPerView="auto" spacing={8}>
+  {productCards.map((card) => (
+    <div key={card.id} className={styles.slide}>
+      {card}
+    </div>
+  ))}
+</Carousel>
+```
+
+---
+
+#### 4.2 Molecules
+
+Components composed of atoms. Have product-domain knowledge and layout logic.
+
+##### RecommendationsProductCard
+
+> **Provisional name.** `RecommendationsProductCard` reflects the team context during the fast-track phase. Once the Design System team promotes this to the public library, it will likely be renamed to a variant of a future `ProductCard` atom (e.g. `ProductCard` with `variant="recommendation"`). The rename will be a breaking change and is tracked in the Jira tech-debt ticket.
+
+```ts
+type UnitPrice = {
+  quantity: number;
+  price: number; 
+};
+
+type RecommendationsProductCardProps = {
+  name: string;
+  linkHref: string;
+  imageSrc: string;
+  imageSrcSet?: string;
+  imageAlt?: string;
+  minQty: number;
+  ratingScore?: number;
+  ratingCount?: number;
+  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];
+  defaultQuoteQty: number;
+  onClick?: React.MouseEventHandler;
+  imageOverlay?: React.ReactNode;
+  children?: React.ReactNode;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+
+```tsx
+// Basic
+<RecommendationsProductCard
+  name="Classic Unisex T-Shirt"
+  linkHref="/products/t-shirt"
+  imageSrc="https://cdn.customink.com/..."
+  minQty={12}
+  ratingScore={4.7}
+  ratingCount={312}
+  defaultUnitPrices={[{ quantity: 12, price: 8.99 }]}
+  defaultQuoteQty={12}
+  onClick={() => trackClick(product)}
+  imageOverlay={<FavoriteButton isFavorited={false} onToggle={handleToggle} />}
+/>
+
+// With extra content via children
+<RecommendationsProductCard
+  name="Classic Unisex T-Shirt"
+  linkHref="/products/t-shirt"
+  imageSrc="https://cdn.customink.com/..."
+  minQty={12}
+  defaultUnitPrices={[{ quantity: 12, price: 8.99 }]}
+  defaultQuoteQty={12}
+>
+  <AddToCartButton productId={product.styleId} />
+</RecommendationsProductCard>
+```
+
+---
+
+##### FavoriteButton
+
+- ![Trending products widget on Favorites page (WIP)](./rfc-assets/1-trending-products-widget-migration-img-5.png)
+
+State (`isFavorited`, `onToggle`) is owned by the consuming app's `FavoritesProvider`. The Pigment atom is purely presentational — renders `HeartIcon` / `HeartFilledIcon`, handles click and keyboard events, delegates upward.
+
+```ts
+type FavoriteButtonProps = {
+  isFavorited: boolean;
+  onToggle: (event: React.MouseEvent | React.KeyboardEvent) => void;
+  showTooltip?: boolean;
+  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
+  'aria-label'?: string;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+
+```tsx
+// Standalone
+<FavoriteButton
+  isFavorited={isFavorited(product.styleId)}
+  onToggle={(e) => {
+    e.preventDefault();
+    e.stopPropagation();
+    toggleFavorite(product.styleId);
+  }}
+  showTooltip
+/>
+
+// Inside RecommendationsProductCard imageOverlay
+// Note: during fast-track phase, FavoriteButton is imported from the local Atoms/ directory,
+// not from the public package. Import path will change once the atom is promoted.
+<RecommendationsProductCard
+  {...cardProps}
+  imageOverlay={
+    <FavoriteButton
+      isFavorited={isFavorited(product.styleId)}
+      onToggle={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggleFavorite(product.styleId); }}
+    />
+  }
+/>
+```
+
+---
+
+##### Tooltip
+
+The v0 `Tooltip` is a one-line MUI passthrough. The v1 uses `@floating-ui/react` for positioning without MUI. Hover and focus triggered, renders in a portal.
+
+```ts
+type TooltipProps = {
+  title: React.ReactNode;
+  children: React.ReactElement;
+  placement?: 'top' | 'top-start' | 'top-end' | 'bottom' | 'bottom-start' | 'bottom-end' | 'left' | 'right';
+  arrow?: boolean;
+  open?: boolean;
+  defaultOpen?: boolean;
+  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+
+```tsx
+<Tooltip title="Add to favorites" placement="bottom">
+  <button aria-label="Favorite">♡</button>
+</Tooltip>
+
+// Controlled
+<Tooltip title="Saved!" open={showConfirmation} placement="top">
+  <FavoriteButton isFavorited onToggle={handleToggle} />
+</Tooltip>
+```
+
+---
+
+##### Popover
+
+Used for pricing detail breakdown. Same `@floating-ui/react` stack as Tooltip, but click-triggered, interactive content, and dismissible on outside click / Escape.
+
+```ts
+type PopoverProps = {
+  trigger: React.ReactElement;
+  children: React.ReactNode;
+  placement?: 'top' | 'bottom' | 'left' | 'right';
+  open?: boolean;
+  defaultOpen?: boolean;
+  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+**Usage:**
+
+```tsx
+<Popover
+  trigger={<button>See pricing details</button>}
+  placement="bottom"
+>
+  <PriceBreakdownTable prices={defaultUnitPrices} />
+</Popover>
+
+// Controlled
+<Popover
+  trigger={<span>{formattedPrice}</span>}
+  open={isPriceOpen}
+  onOpenChange={setIsPriceOpen}
+>
+  <PriceBreakdownTable prices={defaultUnitPrices} />
+</Popover>
+```
+
+---
+
+### Assembled widget
+
+`TrendingProductsWidget` composes the atoms. Data fetching stays in the consuming app — the widget accepts `products` + `isLoading` as props and is unaware of API endpoints.
+
+```ts
+type TrendingProduct = {
+  styleId: number;
+  name: string;
+  link: string;
+  imageSrc: string;
+  imageSrcSet?: string;
+  minQty: number;
+  ratingScore?: number;
+  ratingCount?: number;
+  defaultUnitPrices: { quantity: number; price: number }[];
+  defaultQuoteQty: number;
+  primaryCategoryId: number;
+};
+
+type TrendingProductsWidgetProps = {
+  products: TrendingProduct[];
+  isLoading?: boolean;
+  title?: string;
+  /**
+   * Called once when the widget scrolls into the viewport.
+   * Receives all loaded products so the consumer can build any analytics payload.
+   * @example (products) => gtag('event', 'view_item_list', { items: products.map(...) })
+   */
+  onWidgetViewed?: () => void;
+  /**
+   * Called when an individual product card enters the viewport.
+   * `index` is the 0-based position in the products array.
+   */
+  onCardViewed?: (product: TrendingProduct, index: number) => void;
+  /**
+   * Called when a product card is clicked.
+   * `index` is the 0-based position in the products array.
+   * @example (product, index) => gtag('event', 'select_item', { item_id: product.styleId, index })
+   */
+  onCardClicked?: (product: TrendingProduct, index: number) => void;
+  renderFavoriteButton?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;
+  renderCardContent?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;
+  /**
+   * Rendered below the carousel. Intended for supplemental content such as a
+   * "View all" link. Not rendered during the loading state.
+   */
+  children?: React.ReactNode;
+  className?: string;
+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
+};
+```
+
+Responsive `slidesPerView` logic (preserved from `TrendingCarousel.jsx`):
+
+| Breakpoint | Products ≥ threshold | `slidesPerView` | spacing |
+|------------|----------------------|-----------------|---------|
+| Mobile | ≥ 3 | 1.6 | 8px |
+| Mobile | < 3 | `'auto'` | 8px |
+| Desktop | ≥ 5 | 4 | 16px |
+| Desktop | < 5 | `'auto'` | 16px |
+
+Arrow visibility: arrows are shown only when **all three** conditions are true: `arrows` prop is `true` AND `!isMobile` AND `slidesPerView !== 'auto'`. On mobile, arrows are never rendered regardless of the `arrows` prop. Loading state renders 4-card shimmer skeleton. Returns `null` when `products` is empty.
+
+**Usage in catalog (after migration):**
+
+```tsx
+// catalog: TrendingCarousel.jsx
+// During fast-track: atoms are imported from the widget's local Atoms/ directory.
+// Once atoms are promoted to the public API, imports switch to '@customink/pigment-react'.
+import { TrendingProductsWidget } from '@customink/pigment-react';
+import { FavoriteButton } from '@customink/pigment-react/widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget/Atoms';
+import FavoritesProvider, { PageType } from 'features/favorites/FavoritesProvider';
+import { useFavorites } from 'features/favorites/FavoritesProvider';
+import useTrendingProductsWrapper from 'hooks/queries/useTrendingProductsWrapper';
+
+function TrendingCarouselInner({ categoryId, title, onProductClick }) {
+  const { isLoading, products } = useTrendingProductsWrapper({ categoryId });
+  const { isFavorited, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
+
+  return (
+    <TrendingProductsWidget
+      products={products}
+      isLoading={isLoading}
+      title={title}
+      onWidgetViewed={(products) =>
+        gtag('event', 'view_item_list', {
+          item_list_id: 'trending_products',
+          item_list_name: title,
+        })
+      }
+      onCardClicked={(product, index) =>
+        gtag('event', 'select_item', {
+          item_list_id: 'trending_products',
+          item_list_name: title,
+          item_id: String(product.styleId),
+          item_name: product.name,
+          index,
+        })
+      }
+      renderFavoriteButton={(product) => (
+        <FavoriteButton
+          isFavorited={isFavorited(product.styleId)}
+          onToggle={(e) => {
+            e.preventDefault();
+            e.stopPropagation();
+            toggleFavorite(product.styleId, { productId: product.styleId, source: 'rec_card', widgetType: 'trending' });
+          }}
+        />
+      )}
```

> AGENT

Got it. Here's the key discrepancy:

---

## Old RFC (PR #1066) vs. ADR-20 (PR #1068/#1083)

### Old structure — **wrong**
```
packages/react/src/widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget/
  Atoms/Carousel/
  Atoms/FavoriteButton/
  Atoms/Tooltip/
  Atoms/Popover/
  Molecules/RecommendationsProductCard/
```
- Lives inside `@customink/pigment-react` → coupled to Pigment's release cycle
- Exported from main `packages/react/src/index.ts`
- Consumer imports from `@customink/pigment-react`

### New structure — **correct per ADR-20**
```
packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget/
  TrendingProductsWidget.tsx
  useTrendingProducts.ts          # hooks allowed here — extensions CAN have business logic
  types.ts
  index.ts
  _atoms/Carousel/                # local, NOT exported from public API
  _atoms/FavoriteButton/
  _atoms/Tooltip/
  _atoms/Popover/
  _molecules/RecommendationsProductCard/

packages/extensions/reco/index.ts   # barrel: export { TrendingProductsWidget } from './TrendingProductsWidget'
apps/storybook/stories/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget.stories.tsx
```
- Consumer imports from `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`
- Local atoms graduate to `@customink/pigment-react` when DS promotes them (same backfill process)
- Independent versioning, reco team owns it fully

### One important unlock
The RFC pushed data fetching (`useTrendingProducts`) to the consumer app. ADR-20 explicitly allows hooks/data fetching inside extensions — so the hook **can live in the package** if the team wants it there.

    54→      onClick={onClick}>
    55→      <a href={linkHref}>
    56→        <Box
    57→          className="rpc-image-wrapper"
    58→          sx={{
    59→            '&:before': {
    60→              boxShadow: '0px 0px 60px 24px #f5f5f5 inset',
    61→              content: '""',
    62→              inset: 0,
    63→              mixBlendMode: 'darken',
    64→              position: 'absolute',
    65→              zIndex: 2
    66→            },
    67→            height: {md: 'unset !important', sm: '9.5rem'},
    68→            objectFit: 'cover',
    69→            overflow: 'hidden',
    70→            paddingTop: {md: '100%', sm: '6.5rem'},
    71→            position: 'relative',
    72→            width: {md: '100%', sm: '10.1rem'}
    73→          }}>
    74→          <Box
    75→            component="img"
    76→            sx={{
    77→              height: '100%',
    78→              left: '50%',
    79→              position: 'absolute',
    80→              top: '50%',
    81→              transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)'
    82→            }}
    83→            src={imageUrl}
    84→            srcSet={imageSetUrl}
    85→            alt={name}
    86→          />
    87→          {showFavoritesIcon && (
    88→            <FavoriteButton
    89→              styleId={productId}
    90→              primaryCategoryId={primaryCategoryId}
    91→              categories={categories}
    92→              source="rec_card"
    93→              widgetType={widgetType}
    94→              sx={{
    95→                top: '0.5rem',
    96→                right: '0.5rem',
    97→                zIndex: 3
    98→              }}
    99→            />
   100→          )}
   101→        </Box>
   102→      </a>
   103→      <CardContent
   104→        aria-label={`View details for ${name}`}
   105→        sx={{
   106→          display: 'flex',
   107→          flex: '1',
   108→          flexDirection: 'column',
   109→          gap: '0.3125rem',
   110→          height: '100%',
   111→          justifyContent: {md: 'flex-start', sm: 'center'},
   112→          mt: 0,
   113→          padding: {md: '0.8125rem 1rem !important', sm: '0.5rem'},
   114→          width: '100%',
   115→          ...cardContentSx
   116→        }}>
   117→        <a href={linkHref}>
   118→          <Typography
   119→            data-testid="reco-product-name"
   120→            sx={{
   121→              color: semantics.light.neutral.text.primary,
   122→              display: '-webkit-box',
   123→              fontSize: {md: '0.875rem', sm: '0.8125rem'},
   124→              fontWeight: 700,
   125→              letterSpacing: 0,
   126→              lineHeight: '125%',
   127→              maxHeight: {md: '2.2rem !important', sm: '2rem !important'},
   128→              overflow: 'hidden',
   129→              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
   130→              WebkitBoxOrient: 'vertical',
   131→              WebkitLineClamp: 2
   132→            }}
   133→            title={name}>
   134→            {name}
   135→          </Typography>
   136→        </a>
   137→        {ratingCountNumber > 0 && ratingScoreNumber > 0 && (
   138→          <Stack
   139→            direction="row"
   140→            gap="0.5rem"
   141→            alignItems="center"
   142→            height="1.25rem">
   143→            <ProductCardRating
   144→              count={ratingCountNumber}
   145→              score={ratingScoreNumber}
   146→              generalComments={generalComments}
   147→              name={name}
   148→            />
   149→          </Stack>
   150→        )}
   151→        <ProductCardPriceDetails
   152→          defaultUnitPrices={defaultUnitPrices}
   153→          productId={productId}
   154→          quantity={useNoMinimum ? 1 : defaultQuoteQty}
   155→          minQty={minQty}
   156→          name={name}
   157→          frontColor={1}
   158→          backColor={0}
   159→          isMobile={isMobile}
   160→          hasSinglesEnabledColors={false}
   161→          hasDirtyQuoteQuantity={false}
   162→          specialty={false}
   163→          productColors={[]}
   164→          primaryCategoryId={primaryCategoryId}
   165→        />
   166→        <Box
   167→          sx={{
   168→            display: 'flex',
   169→            flexDirection: 'row',
   170→            lineHeight: '1.25',
   171→            mt: '0.0625rem',
   172→            width: '100%'
   173→          }}>
   174→          <Typography
   175→            data-testid="reco-product-minimum-quantity"
   176→            sx={{
   177→              color: semantics.light.neutral.text.primary,
   178→              fontSize: '0.8125rem'
   179→            }}>
   180→            {minQty === 1 ? 'No Minimum' : `Minimum ${minQty}`}
   181→          </Typography>
   182→        </Box>
   183→      </CardContent>
   184→    </Card>
   185→  );
   186→};
   187→
   188→export default ProductDetailsCard;
   189→

     1→import {
     2→  Box,
     3→  HeartIcon,
     4→  HeartFilledIcon,
     5→  Tooltip
     6→} from '@customink/pigment-react';
     7→import {semantics} from '@customink/design-tokens';
     8→import React, {useMemo} from 'react';
     9→import {useFavorites} from './FavoritesProvider';
    10→import {SxProps} from '@mui/system';
    11→
    12→interface FavoriteButtonProps {
    13→  styleId: number;
    14→  sx?: SxProps;
    15→  showTooltip?: boolean;
    16→  primaryCategoryId: number | null;
    17→  categories: Category[];
    18→  containerSize?: string | number;
    19→  height?: string | number;
    20→  width?: string | number;
    21→  testId?: string;
    22→  source?: string;
    23→  widgetType?: string;
    24→}
    25→
    26→interface Category {
    27→  id: number;
    28→  name: string;
    29→}
    30→
    31→export default function FavoriteButton({
    32→  styleId,
    33→  sx = {},
    34→  showTooltip = false,
    35→  primaryCategoryId,
    36→  categories,
    37→  containerSize = '2.125rem',
    38→  height,
    39→  width,
    40→  testId = 'product-card-favorites-button',
    41→  source,
    42→  widgetType
    43→}: FavoriteButtonProps) {
    44→  const {isFavorited, toggleFavorite} = useFavorites();
    45→  const favorited = isFavorited(styleId);
    46→
    47→  const primaryCategoryName = useMemo(
    48→    () =>
    49→      categories.find((category: Category) => category.id === primaryCategoryId)
    50→        ?.name,
    51→    [categories, primaryCategoryId]
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  const handleClick = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    55→    event.preventDefault();
    56→    event.stopPropagation();
    57→    const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    58→    void toggleFavorite(styleId, {
    59→      position: {top: rect.top, left: rect.right},
    60→      productCategory: primaryCategoryName,
    61→      productId: styleId,
    62→      source,
    63→      widgetType
    64→    });
    65→  };
    66→
    67→  const handleKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    68→    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
    69→      event.preventDefault();
    70→      event.stopPropagation();
    71→      const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    72→      void toggleFavorite(styleId, {
    73→        position: {top: rect.top, left: rect.right},
    74→        productCategory: primaryCategoryName,
    75→        productId: styleId,
    76→        source,
    77→        widgetType
    78→      });
    79→    }
    80→  };
    81→
    82→  const tooltipTitle = favorited ? 'Remove from favorites' : 'Add to favorites';
    83→
    84→  const iconSize = {
    85→    width: width ?? '1.5rem',
    86→    height: height ?? '1.5rem'
    87→  };
    88→
    89→  const button = (
    90→    <Box
    91→      role="button"
    92→      tabIndex={0}
    93→      aria-label={tooltipTitle}
    94→      aria-pressed={favorited}
    95→      data-testid={testId}
    96→      onClick={handleClick}
    97→      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
    98→      sx={{
    99→        position: 'absolute',
   100→        cursor: 'pointer',
   101→        display: 'flex',
   102→        alignItems: 'center',
   103→        justifyContent: 'center',
   104→        width: containerSize,
   105→        height: containerSize,
   106→        padding: 0,
   107→        margin: 0,
   108→        backgroundColor: semantics.light.neutral.background.primary,
   109→        borderRadius: '50%',
   110→        boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
   111→        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
   112→        '&:hover': {
   113→          transform: 'scale(1.1)',
   114→          boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)'
   115→        },
   116→        ...(!favorited && {
   117→          '&:hover svg path': {
   118→            fill: semantics.light.partnership.background.subtleHover,
   119→            stroke: semantics.light.partnership.border.default,
   120→            transition: 'fill 0.2s ease-in-out, stroke 0.2s ease-in-out'
   121→          }
   122→        }),
   123→        ...sx
   124→      }}>
   125→      {favorited ? (
   126→        <HeartFilledIcon
   127→          sx={{
   128→            color: semantics.light.partnership.icon.default,
   129→            ...iconSize
   130→          }}
   131→        />
   132→      ) : (
   133→        <HeartIcon
   134→          sx={{
   135→            color: semantics.light.partnership.border.default,
   136→            ...iconSize
   137→          }}
   138→        />
   139→      )}
   140→    </Box>
   141→  );
   142→
   143→  if (showTooltip) {
   144→    return (
   145→      <Tooltip title={tooltipTitle} placement="bottom" arrow>
   146→        {button}
   147→      </Tooltip>
   148→    );
   149→  }
   150→
   151→  return button;
   152→}
   153→

     1→import {Box, Link} from '@customink/pigment-react';
     2→import {useRollbar} from '@rollbar/react';
     3→import PropTypes from 'prop-types';
     4→import React, {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react';
     5→import {
     6→  unbundledPriceEligibleByCategories,
     7→  unbundledPriceSortEligible
     8→} from 'utils/abTests/unbundledPriceTest';
     9→import useProductCardPrice from '../../hooks/useProductCardPrice';
    10→import ProductCardPriceDetailDialog from './ProductCardPriceDetailDialog';
    11→import ProductCardPriceDetailsContent from './ProductCardPriceDetailsContent';
    12→import ProductCardPricePopover from './ProductCardPricePopover';
    13→import ProductCardRegularPrice from './ProductCardRegularPrice';
    14→import PromoDiscountPrice from './PromoDiscountPrice/PromoDiscountPrice';
    15→import BlankPrice from './BlankPrice';
    16→
    17→export default function ProductCardPriceDetails({
    18→  backColor,
    19→  categories = [],
    20→  categoryId,
    21→  defaultUnitPrices,
    22→  frontColor,
    23→  hasDirtyQuoteQuantity = false,
    24→  hasSinglesEnabledColors,
    25→  isMobile,
    26→  minQty,
    27→  name,
    28→  productColors,
    29→  productId,
    30→  quantity,
    31→  specialty
    32→}) {
    33→  const rollbar = useRollbar();
    34→  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    35→  const [isPopoverOpen, setIsPopoverOpen] = useState(false);
    36→  const anchorRef = useRef(null);
    37→  const closeTimeoutRef = useRef(null);
    38→  const openTimeoutRef = useRef(null);
    39→
    40→  const {
    41→    localizedPrice,
    42→    localizedRegularPrice,
    43→    localizedBlankPrice,
    44→    price,
    45→    regularPrice
    46→  } = useProductCardPrice({
    47→    backColor,
    48→    defaultUnitPrices,
    49→    frontColor,
    50→    hasDirtyQuoteQuantity,
    51→    productId,
    52→    quantity
    53→  });
    54→
    55→  const isUnbundledPriceEligible =
    56→    unbundledPriceSortEligible(categoryId) ||
    57→    unbundledPriceEligibleByCategories(categories);
    58→
    59→  useEffect(() => {
    60→    if (isUnbundledPriceEligible && !localizedBlankPrice) {
    61→      rollbar.warning(
    62→        'Blank price missing for unbundled price eligible product',
    63→        {productId, categoryId}
    64→      );
    65→    }
    66→  }, [
    67→    isUnbundledPriceEligible,
    68→    localizedBlankPrice,
    69→    rollbar,
    70→    productId,
    71→    categoryId
    72→  ]);
    73→
    74→  const hasAlgoliaDiscount =
    75→    regularPrice != null &&
    76→    price != null &&
    77→    Number(regularPrice) > Number(price);
    78→
    79→  const shouldShowDiscount = hasAlgoliaDiscount;
    80→
    81→  // Clean up timeouts on unmount
    82→  useEffect(() => {
    83→    return () => {
    84→      if (closeTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(closeTimeoutRef.current);
    85→      if (openTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(openTimeoutRef.current);
    86→    };
    87→  }, []);
    88→
    89→  const firePricingDetailsTag = useCallback(() => {
    90→    window.dataLayer.push({
    91→      event: 'interaction',
    92→      interaction_action: 'pricing details',
    93→      interaction_category: 'product listings page',
    94→      interaction_label: name
    95→    });
    96→  }, [name]);
    97→
    98→  const quatableMinQty = useMemo(() => {
    99→    return (
   100→      productColors?.find((col) => productId === col.id)?.min_qty || minQty
   101→    );
   102→  }, [minQty, productColors, productId]);
   103→
   104→  const handleMouseEnter = useCallback(() => {
   105→    if (!isMobile) {
   106→      if (closeTimeoutRef.current) {
   107→        clearTimeout(closeTimeoutRef.current);
   108→      }
   109→      if (openTimeoutRef.current) {
   110→        clearTimeout(openTimeoutRef.current);
   111→      }
   112→      openTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   113→        firePricingDetailsTag();
   114→        setIsPopoverOpen(true);
   115→      }, 100);
   116→    }
   117→  }, [firePricingDetailsTag, isMobile]);
   118→
   119→  const handleMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {
   120→    if (!isMobile) {
   121→      if (openTimeoutRef.current) {
   122→        clearTimeout(openTimeoutRef.current);
   123→      }
   124→      closeTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   125→        setIsPopoverOpen(false);
   126→      }, 100);
   127→    }
   128→  }, [isMobile]);
   129→
   130→  const handleClick = (event) => {
   131→    event.preventDefault();
   132→    event.stopPropagation();
   133→    if (isMobile) {
   134→      firePricingDetailsTag();
   135→      setIsModalOpen(true);
   136→    }
   137→  };
   138→
   139→  if (!localizedPrice && !(isUnbundledPriceEligible && localizedBlankPrice)) {
   140→    return null;
   141→  }
   142→
   143→  return (
   144→    <Box
   145→      display="flex"
   146→      data-testid="product-price-details"
   147→      flexDirection={isMobile ? 'column' : 'row'}
   148→      alignItems={isMobile ? 'flex-start' : 'center'}
   149→      justifyContent={isMobile ? 'center' : 'flex-start'}
   150→      flexWrap={isMobile ? 'nowrap' : 'wrap'}
   151→      lineHeight={1.25}
   152→      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   153→      gap="0.125rem 0.5rem">
   154→      {isUnbundledPriceEligible && localizedBlankPrice ? (
   155→        <BlankPrice price={localizedBlankPrice} />
   156→      ) : shouldShowDiscount ? (
   157→        <Box sx={{flexBasis: '100%'}}>
   158→          <PromoDiscountPrice
   159→            discountedPrice={localizedPrice}
   160→            originalPrice={hasAlgoliaDiscount ? localizedRegularPrice : null}
   161→            price={price}
   162→            quantity={quantity}
   163→          />
   164→        </Box>
   165→      ) : (
   166→        <ProductCardRegularPrice price={localizedPrice} quantity={quantity} />
   167→      )}
   168→      {!(isUnbundledPriceEligible && localizedBlankPrice) && (
   169→        <>
   170→          <Link
   171→            component="span"
   172→            sx={{
   173→              fontSize: '0.75rem',
   174→              fontWeight: '500'
   175→            }}
   176→            ref={anchorRef}
   177→            onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
   178→            onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
   179→            onClick={handleClick}>
   180→            Pricing Details
   181→          </Link>
   182→          <Box>
   183→            {isMobile && (
   184→              <ProductCardPriceDetailDialog
   185→                title={name}
   186→                productId={productId}
   187→                open={isModalOpen}
   188→                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}>
   189→                <ProductCardPriceDetailsContent

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/pigment/docs/plans/RECO-730-trending-products-migration.md",
  "content": "# RECO-730: TrendingProductsWidget Migration Plan\n\n**Branch:** `RECO-730-trendng-products-migration`\n**Package:** `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`\n**Source:** `catalog/app/javascript/features/TrendingProducts/TrendingCarousel.jsx`\n**References:** [ADR-20](../adr/20-Extensions-Package.md) · [RFC-1](../rfc/1-trending-products-widget-migration.md) · PR #1068 · PR #1083\n\n---\n\n## Scope Summary\n\nMigrate `TrendingCarousel.jsx` from the catalog repo into `packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget/` using the ADR-20 extensions structure. The widget is broken into internal atoms/molecules (not publicly exported) and one assembled widget component + one dumb hook (publicly exported).\n\n---\n\n## What Does NOT Move\n\n| Catalog piece | Why | How replaced |\n|---|---|---|\n| `FavoritesProvider` / `useFavorites` | Auth-aware, catalog-specific context | Catalog wraps widget with `FavoritesProvider`; injects button via `renderFavoriteButton` render prop |\n| `ProductCardPriceDetails` | Deeply catalog-specific: Rollbar, AB tests, `useProductCardPriceDetails` data fetching | `RecommendationsProductCard` exposes `renderPriceDetails` render prop |\n| `useTrendingProductsWrapper` / `usePromotionalProducts` | React Query, catalog API config | Replaced by dumb `useTrendingProducts` hook that accepts a `fetcher` function |\n| `firePromoTrendingRecommendationClickEvent` / `ViewEvent` | Catalog analytics utils | Replaced by `onWidgetViewed` / `onCardViewed` / `onCardClicked` callback props on the widget |\n| `useBreakpoint` (catalog hook) | Catalog-specific | Re-implement as local `useMediaQuery`-based hook inside widget |\n| MUI `styled()` | v0 pattern, no MUI in new code | CSS Modules |\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\npackages/extensions/reco/\n  TrendingProductsWidget/\n    TrendingProductsWidget.tsx          # assembled widget, public\n    TrendingProductsWidget.module.css\n    TrendingProductsWidget.test.tsx\n    types.ts                            # all TypeScript contracts\n    index.ts                            # public exports only\n    Atoms/                              # internal, NOT exported from index.ts\n      Carousel/\n        Carousel.tsx\n        Carousel.module.css\n        index.ts\n      FavoriteButton/\n        FavoriteButton.tsx\n        FavoriteButton.module.css\n        index.ts\n      Tooltip/\n        Tooltip.tsx                     # thin wrapper over pigment-react v0 Tooltip (fast-track)\n        index.ts\n      LoadingSkeleton/\n        LoadingSkeleton.tsx\n        LoadingSkeleton.module.css\n        index.ts\n    Molecules/\n      RecommendationsProductCard/\n        RecommendationsProductCard.tsx\n        RecommendationsProductCard.module.css\n        RecommendationsProductCard.test.tsx\n        index.ts\n  useTrendingProducts/\n    useTrendingProducts.ts              # dumb hook, public\n    useTrendingProducts.test.ts\n    index.ts\n  index.ts                             # reco barrel export\n\napps/storybook/stories/extensions/reco/\n  TrendingProductsWidget.stories.tsx\n```\n\n---\n\n## Dependency Audit (DS Review Required per ADR-20)\n\n| Dep | Status | Action |\n|---|---|---|\n| `keen-slider` | Already in catalog, not in extensions | Add to `packages/extensions/package.json`. **Flag for DS review before merging.** |\n| `@floating-ui/react` | Not needed phase 1 — use v0 Tooltip | Defer. Add when DS ships official v1 Tooltip/Popover. |\n\n---\n\n## TypeScript Contracts (`types.ts`)\n\n```ts\nexport type UnitPrice = {\n  quantity: number;\n  price: number;\n};\n\nexport type TrendingProduct = {\n  styleId: number;\n  name: string;\n  link: string;\n  imageSrc: string;\n  imageSrcSet?: string;\n  imageAlt?: string;\n  minQty: number;\n  ratingScore?: number;\n  ratingCount?: number;\n  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];\n  defaultQuoteQty: number;\n  primaryCategoryId: number;\n};\n\nexport type TrendingProductsWidgetProps = {\n  products: TrendingProduct[];\n  isLoading?: boolean;\n  title?: string;\n  onWidgetViewed?: (products: TrendingProduct[]) => void;\n  onCardViewed?: (product: TrendingProduct, index: number) => void;\n  onCardClicked?: (product: TrendingProduct, index: number) => void;\n  /** Render prop: inject FavoriteButton from catalog (has FavoritesProvider context) */\n  renderFavoriteButton?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;\n  /** Render prop: inject ProductCardPriceDetails from catalog */\n  renderPriceDetails?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;\n  children?: React.ReactNode;\n  className?: string;\n  style?: React.CSSProperties;\n};\n```\n\n---\n\n## Component Contracts\n\n### Carousel (internal Atom)\n\n```ts\ntype CarouselProps = {\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  slidesPerView?: number | 'auto';\n  spacing?: number;\n  loop?: boolean;\n  arrows?: boolean;\n  onPreviousClick?: () => void;\n  onNextClick?: () => void;\n  className?: string;\n  style?: React.CSSProperties;\n};\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Direct port from `TrendingCarousel.jsx` keen-slider logic\n- Replaces MUI `styled` with CSS modules\n- Replaces `semantics.*` hardcoded values with `var(--token-*)` CSS custom properties\n- Arrow buttons: use `IconButton` + `ArrowDownIcon` from `@customink/pigment-react` (these are v1 already)\n- `useResizeObserver` (`@react-hook/resize-observer`) — check if already in extensions deps; if not, inline a `ResizeObserver` effect\n- `forwardRef` + `displayName` required (Pigment v1 pattern)\n\n### FavoriteButton (internal Atom)\n\n```ts\ntype FavoriteButtonProps = {\n  isFavorited: boolean;\n  onToggle: (event: React.MouseEvent | React.KeyboardEvent) => void;\n  showTooltip?: boolean;\n  size?: 'sm' | 'md';\n  'aria-label'?: string;\n  className?: string;\n  style?: React.CSSProperties;\n};\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Port visual design from `catalog/features/favorites/FavoriteButton.tsx`\n- Remove `useFavorites()` — state is fully external (isFavorited + onToggle as props)\n- Remove `SxProps` / MUI — CSS modules\n- Keep `HeartIcon` / `HeartFilledIcon` from `@customink/pigment-react`\n- Keep `Tooltip` wrapper for `showTooltip` — use local `Tooltip` atom (v0 passthrough)\n- `forwardRef` + `displayName`\n\n### Tooltip (internal Atom, fast-track)\n\n```ts\n// Thin wrapper over v0 Tooltip from @customink/pigment-react\n// No reimplementation needed — swap import when DS ships v1\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Import `Tooltip` from `@customink/pigment-react` and re-export as-is\n- Single file, no CSS module needed\n- Add Jira tech-debt ticket\n\n### LoadingSkeleton (internal Atom)\n\n```ts\ntype LoadingSkeletonProps = {\n  className?: string;\n  style?: React.CSSProperties;\n};\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Port `CarouselSkeleton` from `catalog/features/TrendingProducts/components/LoadingSkeleton.jsx`\n- Remove MUI `styled` / `keyframes` — use CSS modules with `@keyframes`\n- Replace hardcoded colors with `var(--token-*)` CSS custom properties\n- 4-card shimmer grid layout\n\n### RecommendationsProductCard (internal Molecule)\n\n```ts\ntype RecommendationsProductCardProps = {\n  name: string;\n  linkHref: string;\n  imageSrc: string;\n  imageSrcSet?: string;\n  imageAlt?: string;\n  minQty: number;\n  ratingScore?: number;\n  ratingCount?: number;\n  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];\n  defaultQuoteQty: number;\n  onClick?: React.MouseEventHandler;\n  /** Slot for FavoriteButton overlay (positioned top-right of image) */\n  imageOverlay?: React.ReactNode;\n  /** Slot for custom price rendering (catalog injects ProductCardPriceDetails) */\n  renderPriceDetails?: () => React.ReactNode;\n  children?: React.ReactNode;\n  className?: string;\n  style?: React.CSSProperties;\n};\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Port visual structure from `catalog/product_details_page/RelatedProducts/RelatedProductCard.jsx`\n- Use `Card`, `CardContent`, `Typography`, `Stack`, `Box`, `Link` from `@customink/pigment-react` (v0 backward-compat is fine for fast-track)\n- Remove `ProductCardPriceDetails` — render `renderPriceDetails?.()` in its place, or fall back to a basic price display from `defaultUnitPrices[0]` if no render prop given\n- Remove `FavoriteButton` import — render `imageOverlay` prop in position: absolute top-right slot\n- Remove catalog-specific: `processImageUrl`, `searchParams`, `useMobileBreakpoint` (use local CSS / media query)\n- Basic rating display: star score + count (can use `Typography` for now, or port `ProductCardRating`)\n- `forwardRef` + `displayName`\n- CSS modules, no MUI sx, no hardcoded colors\n\n### ProductCardRating (internal, inside RecommendationsProductCard or standalone)\n\n- Port from `catalog/features/products/components/ProductCard/components/ProductCardRating`\n- Or: render rating as plain `Typography` in phase 1, extract to atom later\n\n---\n\n## useTrendingProducts Hook (public)\n\n```ts\ntype TrendingProductsFetcher = () => Promise<TrendingProduct[]>;\n\ntype UseTrendingProductsOptions = {\n  fetcher: TrendingProductsFetcher;\n  enabled?: boolean;\n};\n\ntype UseTrendingProductsResult = {\n  products: TrendingProduct[];\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  isError: boolean;\n};\n\nfunction useTrendingProducts(options: UseTrendingProductsOptions): UseTrendingProductsResult;\n```\n\n**Implementation notes:**\n- Dumb hook: calls `fetcher()`, manages `loading`/`error`/`data` state with `useEffect` + `useState`\n- No React Query, no catalog API wiring — those stay in catalog\n- No in-memory cache for phase 1 (simple `useEffect`, re-fetches on mount)\n- `enabled` flag: skip fetch when `false` (matches catalog `useTrendingProducts` pattern)\n- Returns empty `products: []` on error (same behavior as current catalog hook)\n\n**Catalog usage:**\n```ts\n// catalog: useTrendingProductsWrapper.js wraps this\nconst { products, isLoading } = useTrendingProducts({\n  fetcher: () => fetchPromotionalProducts({ categoryId, limit: 20 }),\n  enabled: true,\n});\n```\n\n---\n\n## TrendingProductsWidget Assembly\n\n**Responsive slidesPerView logic** (preserve from `TrendingCarousel.jsx`):\n\n| Breakpoint | Products count | slidesPerView | spacing |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Mobile (`≤ 768px`) | ≥ 3 | 1.6 | 8px |\n| Mobile | < 3 | `'auto'` | 8px |\n| Desktop | ≥ 5 | 4 | 16px |\n| Desktop | < 5 | `'auto'` | 16px |\n\n**Arrow visibility:** `showArrows = slidesPerView !== 'auto' && !isMobile`\n\n**Widget behavior:**\n- `isLoading` → render `LoadingSkeleton`\n- `products.length === 0` → return `null`\n- Viewport visibility: `IntersectionObserver` on wrapper → `onWidgetViewed(products)` fires once\n- Card click → `onCardClicked(product, index)`\n- Card in viewport → `onCardViewed(product, index)` (per-card IntersectionObserver, optional phase 2)\n\n**`FavoritesProvider` is NOT wrapped here** — catalog is responsible for wrapping:\n```tsx\n// catalog (after migration)\n<FavoritesProvider pageType={PageType.SUBCATEGORY}>\n  <TrendingProductsWidget\n    products={products}\n    isLoading={isLoading}\n    renderFavoriteButton={(product) => (\n      <FavoriteButton\n        styleId={product.styleId}\n        isFavorited={isFavorited(product.styleId)}\n        onToggle={...}\n      />\n    )}\n    renderPriceDetails={(product) => (\n      <ProductCardPriceDetails\n        defaultUnitPrices={product.defaultUnitPrices}\n        productId={product.styleId}\n        quantity={product.defaultQuoteQty}\n        {...catalogSpecificProps}\n      />\n    )}\n  />\n</FavoritesProvider>\n```\n\n---\n\n## Public Exports (`reco/index.ts`)\n\n```ts\nexport { TrendingProductsWidget } from './TrendingProductsWidget';\nexport { useTrendingProducts } from './useTrendingProducts';\nexport type { TrendingProduct, TrendingProductsWidgetProps } from './TrendingProductsWidget/types';\n```\n\nAtoms and Molecules are **NOT exported**. No deep imports from catalog.\n\n---\n\n## Storybook Story\n\nLocation: `apps/storybook/stories/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget.stories.tsx`\n\nStories to cover:\n1. `Default` — 5+ products, no favorites, no custom price\n2. `Loading` — `isLoading=true`, shows skeleton\n3. `FewProducts` — 2 products, `slidesPerView='auto'`\n4. `WithFavoriteButton` — `renderFavoriteButton` injected (mock state)\n5. `Mobile` — responsive viewport story\n\nUse static mock data. No real API calls in Storybook.\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Phases\n\n### Phase 0 — Dependency audit (before coding)\n- [ ] Open DS review request for `keen-slider` in `packages/extensions/package.json`\n- [ ] Confirm `@react-hook/resize-observer` is already available or inline a `ResizeObserver` effect\n\n### Phase 1 — Types + Internal atoms (parallel)\n- [ ] `types.ts`\n- [ ] `Atoms/Carousel/` (keen-slider, CSS modules, arrows)\n- [ ] `Atoms/FavoriteButton/` (presentational, no context)\n- [ ] `Atoms/Tooltip/` (v0 passthrough)\n- [ ] `Atoms/LoadingSkeleton/` (CSS modules shimmer)\n\n### Phase 2 — Molecule\n- [ ] `Molecules/RecommendationsProductCard/` (with `imageOverlay` + `renderPriceDetails` slots)\n\n### Phase 3 — Hook\n- [ ] `useTrendingProducts` (dumb, fetcher injection)\n- [ ] Unit tests\n\n### Phase 4 — Widget assembly\n- [ ] `TrendingProductsWidget.tsx` (compose atoms + molecule, viewport observer, responsive logic)\n- [ ] `TrendingProductsWidget.test.tsx`\n\n### Phase 5 — Exports + Stories\n- [ ] `reco/index.ts` barrel\n- [ ] Storybook stories\n\n### Phase 6 — Catalog integration (separate PR in catalog)\n- [ ] Replace `TrendingCarousel.jsx` usage with import from `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`\n- [ ] Update `useTrendingProductsWrapper` to pass fetcher to `useTrendingProducts`\n- [ ] Wrap with `FavoritesProvider` + inject `renderFavoriteButton`\n- [ ] Inject `renderPriceDetails`\n\n---\n\n## Tech Debt Tickets to Create (one per component)\n\n| Component | Tier | When to graduate |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Carousel` | Atom | When DS ships v1 Carousel |\n| `FavoriteButton` | Atom | When DS ships v1 FavoriteButton |\n| `Tooltip` | Atom (v0 wrapper) | When DS ships v1 Tooltip |\n| `RecommendationsProductCard` | Molecule | When DS ships `ProductCard` atom |\n\n---\n\n## DS Enforcement Checklist (per ADR-20)\n\n- [ ] No hardcoded color/spacing values — use `var(--token-*)` CSS custom properties only\n- [ ] `@customink/pigment-react` used as peer dep (not bundled)\n- [ ] Storybook story exists for `TrendingProductsWidget`\n- [ ] `keen-slider` dep reviewed by DS before merge\n- [ ] `npm run lint+format:fix` passes\n- [ ] `npm run validate` exits 0\n- [ ] Storybook starts and renders widget story without errors\n"
}

> AGENT

Plan saved to `docs/plans/RECO-730-trending-products-migration.md`.

Key decisions captured vs what the RFC had wrong:

**Structure fix (RFC → ADR-20):**
- RFC: `packages/react/src/widgets/RecommendationsWidgets/TrendingProductsWidget/`
- Correct: `packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget/`

**Three things the RFC didn't account for that I added from reading the actual code:**

1. **`renderPriceDetails` render prop** — `ProductCardPriceDetails` is deeply catalog-specific (Rollbar, AB tests, `useProductCardPriceDetails` data fetching). It can't move. The card exposes a slot instead.

2. **`FavoritesProvider` stays in catalog** — `FavoriteButton.tsx` calls `useFavorites()` context. The widget gets a `renderFavoriteButton` render prop instead. Catalog wraps with `FavoritesProvider` externally.

3. **Dumb hook with fetcher injection** — `useTrendingProducts({ fetcher: () => Promise<TrendingProduct[]> })`. No React Query in extensions. Catalog passes its own `fetchPromotionalProducts` call as the fetcher.

**Tooltip** — no need to reimplement with floating-ui in phase 1. v0 Tooltip from `pigment-react` works today, graduate when DS ships v1.

**Only new dep needing DS review:** `keen-slider` (already in catalog, just needs to be declared in extensions package.json).

Ready for implementation — what's next?

> AGENT

`Tooltip` is only in `v0/index.ts` — it's **not exported from the main `packages/react/src/index.ts`**, meaning it's not part of the public `@customink/pigment-react` API.

So my statement was wrong. The catalog's `FavoriteButton.tsx` imports `Tooltip` directly from `@customink/pigment-react` but that only works because of the v0 backward-compat re-export path — which is internal and not guaranteed stable.

For extensions, we can't rely on that. The correct approach:

- **Phase 1 fast-track:** Implement `Tooltip` locally in `Atoms/Tooltip/` using `@floating-ui/react` — this is a new dep that needs DS review, same as `keen-slider`.
- Or: skip Tooltip entirely for phase 1 — `FavoriteButton.showTooltip` defaults to `false`, so it's optional. Ship without tooltip support, add it in a follow-up.

I'll update the plan to reflect this:
