```typescript
import React from 'react';
import styles from './LoadingSkeleton.module.css';

export type LoadingSkeletonProps = {
  /** Additional CSS class name(s) */
  className?: string;
  /** Inline style overrides */
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
};

/**
 * LoadingSkeleton — shimmer placeholder for the TrendingProductsWidget carousel.
 *
 * Renders a header placeholder and four card placeholders in a responsive grid.
 * Ported from the CarouselSkeleton in catalog's TrendingProducts feature.
 */
export const LoadingSkeleton = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, LoadingSkeletonProps>(
  function LoadingSkeleton({ className, style }, ref) {
    const rootClass = [styles.root, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

    return (
      <div ref={ref} className={rootClass} style={style} data-testid="carousel-skeleton">
        <div className={styles.header} />
        <div className={styles.cards}>
          {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className={styles.card}>
              <div className={styles.cardContent}>
                <div className={styles.cardTitle} />
                <div className={styles.cardPrice} />
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  },
);

LoadingSkeleton.displayName = 'LoadingSkeleton';
```

## 5. RecommendationsProductCard.tsx

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Box, Link, Stack, Typography } from '@customink/pigment-react';
import type { UnitPrice } from '../../types';
import styles from './RecommendationsProductCard.module.css';

// Star path inlined from v0 HeartFilledIcon — v0 icons are not on the public API
const STAR_FILLED_PATH =
  'M11.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.519 4.674a1 1 0 00.95.69h4.915c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-3.976 2.888a1 1 0 00-.363 1.118l1.518 4.674c.3.922-.755 1.688-1.538 1.118l-3.976-2.888a1 1 0 00-1.176 0l-3.976 2.888c-.783.57-1.838-.197-1.538-1.118l1.518-4.674a1 1 0 00-.363-1.118l-3.976-2.888c-.783-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h4.914a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.519-4.674z';

function formatRatingScore(score: number): string {
  const adjusted = score < 1 ? 1 : score;
  return adjusted.toFixed(1);
}

function formatRatingCount(count: number): string | null {
  if (Number.isNaN(count)) return null;
  const cap = 10_000;
  const capped = count > cap ? cap : count;
  const formatted = capped.toLocaleString();
  return `(${count > cap ? `${formatted}+` : formatted})`;
}

function formatPrice(unitPrices: UnitPrice[], quoteQty: number): string | null {
  if (!unitPrices.length) return null;
  const match = unitPrices.find((p) => p.quantity === quoteQty) ?? unitPrices[0];
  return `$${match.price.toFixed(2)}`;
}

export type RecommendationsProductCardProps = {
  name: string;
  linkHref: string;
  imageSrc: string;
  imageSrcSet?: string;
  /** @default name */
  imageAlt?: string;
  minQty: number;
  ratingScore?: number;
  ratingCount?: number;
  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];
  defaultQuoteQty: number;
  onClick?: React.MouseEventHandler;
  /** Slot rendered as an absolute overlay on the product image (e.g. FavoriteButton) */
  imageOverlay?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Render prop for custom price display.
   * Falls back to a simple formatted price from defaultUnitPrices when omitted.
   */
  renderPriceDetails?: () => React.ReactNode;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
};

/**
 * RecommendationsProductCard — internal molecule for TrendingProductsWidget.
 * Provisional name: will be renamed when DS promotes a public ProductCard atom.
 *
 * NOT exported from the reco public API.
 */
export const RecommendationsProductCard = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  RecommendationsProductCardProps
>(function RecommendationsProductCard(
  {
    name,
    linkHref,
    imageSrc,
    imageSrcSet,
    imageAlt,
    minQty,
    ratingScore,
    ratingCount,
    defaultUnitPrices,
    defaultQuoteQty,
    onClick,
    imageOverlay,
    renderPriceDetails,
    children,
    className,
    style,
  },
  ref,
) {
  const hasRating =
    ratingScore != null &&
    ratingCount != null &&
    ratingScore > 0 &&
    ratingCount > 0;

  const cardClass = [styles.card, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className={cardClass} style={style} onClick={onClick}>
      {/* Image area */}
      <a href={linkHref} className={styles.imageLink} tabIndex={-1} aria-hidden="true">
        <div className={styles.imageWrapper}>
          {/* Inset shadow overlay to blend image edges */}
          <div className={styles.imageShadow} aria-hidden="true" />
          <img
            src={imageSrc}
            srcSet={imageSrcSet}
            alt={imageAlt ?? name}
            className={styles.image}
          />
          {imageOverlay}
        </div>
      </a>

      {/* Card content */}
      <div className={styles.content}>
        <a href={linkHref} className={styles.nameLink}>
          <Typography className={styles.name} title={name}>
            {name}
          </Typography>
        </a>

        {hasRating ? <Stack direction="row" className={styles.ratingRow}>
            <svg
              width={20}
              height={20}
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
              aria-hidden="true"
              className={styles.starIcon}
            >
              <path fillRule="evenodd" clipRule="evenodd" d={STAR_FILLED_PATH} fill="currentColor" />
            </svg>
            <Typography className={styles.ratingScore}>
              {formatRatingScore(ratingScore!)}
            </Typography>
            <span className={styles.ratingCount}>
              {formatRatingCount(ratingCount!)}
            </span>
          </Stack> : null}

        <div className={styles.priceArea}>
          {renderPriceDetails
            ? renderPriceDetails()
            : (
              <Typography className={styles.price}>
                {formatPrice(defaultUnitPrices, defaultQuoteQty)}
              </Typography>
            )}
        </div>

        <Typography className={styles.minQty}>
          {minQty === 1 ? 'No Minimum' : `Minimum ${minQty}`}
        </Typography>

        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});

RecommendationsProductCard.displayName = 'RecommendationsProductCard';

     3→import path from 'path';
     4→
     5→const config: StorybookConfig = {
     6→  staticDirs: ['../public'],
     7→  stories: ['../stories/**/*.@(mdx|stories.@(ts|tsx))'],
     8→  core: {
     9→    disableTelemetry: true,
    10→  },
    11→  addons: [
    12→    '@storybook/addon-docs',
    13→    '@storybook/addon-links',
    14→    'storybook-addon-pseudo-states',
    15→    '@storybook/addon-designs',
    16→    '@storybook/addon-a11y',
    17→    '@storybook/addon-webpack5-compiler-babel',
    18→  ],
    19→  framework: '@storybook/react-webpack5',
    20→  webpackFinal: async (config) => {
    21→    if (config.resolve) {
    22→      config.resolve.plugins = [
    23→        ...(config.resolve.plugins || []),
    24→        new TsconfigPathsPlugin({
    25→          extensions: config.resolve.extensions,
    26→        }),
    27→      ];
    28→    }
    29→
    30→    // Configure CSS modules for v1 components with PostCSS
    31→    const reactPackagePath = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../packages/react');
    32→    const extensionsPackagePath = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../packages/extensions');
    33→    const storybookPath = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
    34→    const postcssConfigPath = path.join(reactPackagePath, 'postcss.config.js');
    35→
    36→    // First, exclude .module.css from existing CSS rules to prevent double processing
    37→    config.module?.rules?.forEach((rule) => {
    38→      if (rule && typeof rule === 'object' && 'test' in rule) {
    39→        const testString = rule.test?.toString();
    40→        if (testString?.includes('.css')) {
    41→          // Add exclude for .module.css files
    42→          if (!rule.exclude) {
    43→            rule.exclude = /\.module\.css$/;
    44→          }
    45→        }
    46→      }
    47→    });
    48→
    49→    // Add CSS modules rule with higher priority (before other CSS rules)
    50→    // Include both react package sources and storybook stories
    51→    config.module?.rules?.unshift({
    52→      test: /\.module\.css$/,
    53→      include: [path.join(reactPackagePath, 'src'), path.join(extensionsPackagePath), path.join(storybookPath, 'stories')],
    54→      use: [
    55→        'style-loader',
    56→        {
    57→          loader: 'css-loader',
    58→          options: {
    59→            modules: {
    60→              localIdentName: '[name]__[local]___[hash:base64:5]',
    61→            },
    62→            importLoaders: 1,
    63→          },
    64→        },
    65→        {
    66→          loader: 'postcss-loader',
    67→          options: {
    68→            postcssOptions: {
    69→              config: postcssConfigPath,
    70→            },
    71→          },
    72→        },
    73→      ],
    74→    });
    75→
    76→    return config;
    77→  },

Rambler Insulated Tumbler with MagSlider Lid',\n    imageSrc:\n      'https://mms-images.out.customink.com/mms/images/catalog/00d3cd88ce1d5e833192e60742579b63/colors/2414110/views/alt/front_medium_extended.png?autoNegate=1&ixbg=%23ffffff&ixfm=jpeg&ixq=60&ixw=270&placeMax=1&placeMaxPct=0.8&placeUseProduct=1&placeUseView=front',\n    minQty: 6,\n    ratingScore: 4.94,\n    ratingCount: 12,\n    defaultUnitPrices: [{ quantity: 7, price: 51.35 }],\n    defaultQuoteQty: 72,\n  },\n};\n\nexport const API: Story = {\n  args: BASE_ARGS,\n};\n"

> AGENT

The keen-slider slide has `overflow: hidden` which clips the card's bottom border right at the slide boundary. The fix is to replace `border` with `box-shadow` — box-shadow is not clipped by parent overflow.

> TOOL
